grad_v_j = ∑_i dL/dx_i ∂x_i/∂v_j = 2[ h_j - (x_j/T)∑_i h_i x_i ].

Let's implement an LP step with normalization target T. Pseudocode:

```
def lp_direction(x, M, max_obj? time_limit?)
    n=x.size; T=x.sum()
    U = max(10.0, 10*max(x)) # maybe also M/min(x)
    # Build sparse A
    ...
    b = M * (1 + 1e-8) + 1e-8
    res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,U)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':'auto'})
    if success:
       y=...
       sy=y.sum()
       if sy > T*(1+1e-6):
           ynorm = y*T/sy
           return ynorm, sy
    return None
```

If `linprog` not available? scipy import. Could fallback to custom block coordinate? But SciPy allowed.

```
def lp_update(x, M):
   y=lp_direction(...)
   if y None: return x,M
   bestx=x; bestM=M
   # alpha set: 1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02
   for alpha in [...]:
       z=(1-alpha)x+alpha*y
       z = z / z.sum()*T # ensure
       m=peak_score_scaled(z)
       if m<bestM: best=z,m
   return best
```

Let's derive the SOTA LP more rigorously to ensure correct normalization.
Given nonnegative f length n normalized sum=√(2n). M=||f*f||∞. Solve LP:
max ∑ b_j subject b_j≥0 and ||f*g0||∞≤M. Then rescale g0 to sum=√2n: g=S/sum(b)b. Update f←(1-t)f+t g.
Why does this reduce M? Let's test with current f and y=LP optimum. At current f, for active k where f*f=M, derivative of (f+ty)*(f+ty) =2t f*y + t^2 y*y. Since f*y≤M=f*f_active, derivative first order ≤2M? But need relative to M? For active constraints, (f+ty)*(f+ty)_k = M +2t(f*y_k - M)+t^2 y*y_k = M -? If f*y≤M, first term nonpositive. If f*y=M (for y near feasible boundary), second order can violate for t>0. But after rescale g maybe lower. Convex combo with t small maybe. Frank-Wolfe likely works.

Could we instead solve LP with constraints f*y≤M - δ to ensure descent? Not.

Maybe the objective should use f normalized so ∑f=1, M full. LP maximize ∑y s.t. f*y ≤ M (current). The feasible set includes y=f (sum1). If sum_y>1, normalized y has lower some measure. ```
def build_conv_A(x):
 n=len(x)
 rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
 for k in range(2*n-1):
   if k < n:
     m=k+1
     cols.append(np.arange(m))
     data.append(x[k::-1]) # length m
   else:
     m=2*n-1-k
     cols.append(np.arange(n-m,n)) # k-n+1 to n-1
     data.append(x[n-1::-1][:m])
   rows.append(np.full(m,k))
 return coo_matrix((np.concatenate(data),(np.concatenate(rows),np.concatenate(cols))), shape=(2*n-1,n)).tocsr()
```

Now `soft_fjac(v,beta)` details with FFT:
```
def soft_fjac(v, beta):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    # handle n=0? not used
    T = math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    # stabilize
    vmax = float(np.max(v)) if n>0 else 0
    ex = np.exp(v - vmax)
    p = ex / ex.sum()
    x = T * p
    L_fft = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    # maybe ensure L_fft >= 2*n-1
    Fx = np.fft.rfft(x, n=L_fft)
    c_full = np.fft.irfft(Fx * Fx, n=L_fft)
    c = c_full[:2*n-1].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not finite: return inf, zeros
    z = beta*(c-cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -745, 0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    ez_sum = float(np.sum(ez))
    if ez_sum<=0 or not finite: ...
    L = cmax + math.log(ez_sum)/beta
    w = ez/ez_sum
    # h
    Ar = np.zeros(L_fft)
    Ar[:n] = x[::-1]
    FAr = np.fft.rfft(Ar, n=L_fft)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L_fft)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(FAr*Fw, n=L_fft)
    h = h_full[n-1:2*n-1].real
    h[~finite]=0
    Wc = float(np.dot(c, w)) # or sum c*w
    grad = 2.0*(h - (x/T)*Wc) # x/T=p
    # gradient should sum zero; numerical: ensure
    grad = grad - grad.mean()
    if not finite: ...
    return L, grad
```
Check gradient of L (not including 2n factor). The objective with softmax x sum T is L (approx max c). If we want exact score, use factor T? T^2=2n so score = 2n L/S^2 = L. Yes. But if due normalization x sum exactly T? p sum1. Good. The continuous optimum of LSE(β) maybe at T. If beta, L approximates max c; we can also scale objective by maybe 1? central score = max c =~1.57. Soft objective value ~L, good.

Need check gradient formula: x_i = T p_i. L depends on x. dL/dx_i = 2h_i. d p_i/dv_j = p_i(δ_{ij} - p_j). d x_i/dv_j = T p_i(δ - p_j) = x_i(δ - p_j). Then grad_j = ∑_i 2h_i * x_j(δ? Wait for each i, derivative of x_i wrt v_j = x_i(δ_{ij} - p_j) = x_i(δ_{ij} - x_j/T). Sum_i 2h_i x_i(δ_{ij} - x_j/T) = 2(h_j x_j - x_j∑h_i x_i/T). Wait I missed factor x_j? Let's recalc: h index is derivative wrt x_k: dL/dx_k=2h_k. Then grad_v_j = ∑_k (2h_k) * ∂x_k/∂v_j. ∂x_k/∂v_j = x_k(δ_{kj} - x_j/T). So grad_j = 2[ h_j x_j - x_j ∑_k h_k x_k / T ]. Ah there is extra x_j. Earlier I wrote h_j without x_j. Mist! Let's derive alternative using p:
x_k=T p_k, dL/dp_k = T dL/dx_k =2T h_k. d p_k/dv_j = p_k(δ_{kj}-p_j). grad_v_j = ∑ 2T h_k p_k(δ - p_j)=2T[ h_j p_j - p_j ∑ h_k p_k] = 2 x_j [ h_j - (x_j/T)∑h_k x_k? Wait ∑ h_k p_k = ∑ h_k x_k/T.    T=√(2n); best_x=normalize_T(x)
   best_score=score_exact(best_x) maybe fast then exact; use exact for central? Use score_fast for cheap? Score exact O(n^2) once okay. For n=4096 exact 16M maybe 0.1s; okay.
   v=to_v(best_x) - mean

         v_new, val, ok = lbfgs_chunk(v,beta, chunk_iters=40, deadline)
         # if ok and improves? get x_new=from_v(v_new)

      if time done break

Now `lp_refine_loop`:
```
def lp_refine_loop(x, deadline, max_iters):
   best_x=x; best_score=score_fast(x)
   best_score_exact=score_exact(x)
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > deadline - 5: break
       M = max_conv_exact(best_x) # exact for constraints
       if not finite: break
       y = lp_direction(best_x, M, deadline-it)
       if y is None: break
       z, sc_fast = lp_line_search(best_x, y)
       if sc_fast is finite and sc_fast < best_score - 1e-9:
           sc_exact = score_exact(z)
           if sc_exact < best_score_exact - 1e-10:
               best_x=z; best_score_exact=sc_exact; best_score=sc_fast; no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if no_improve >= 5: break
   return best_x,best_score_exact
```
Max iters: for n=1024 maybe 20; 2048 10; 4096 5. But time drives.

  shapes.append(c)
  shapes.append(c[::-1]) # same score but shape
  shapes.append((c+c[::-1])/2)
  # power α
  for α in [0.4,0.45,0.5]:
    i=np.arange(1,n+1); a=i**(-α); shapes.append(a)
  # central with edge reduction: a=exp(log(c)*(1+λ?) or c/(i+1)^β?

  shapes.append(c + 0.5*(np.ones(n)-c.mean?) maybe)

  # random multiplicative noise on central

def smooth_refine(x, deadline, rng=None):
   T=...
   cur=normalize_T(x); cur_score=score_fast(cur)
   best=cur; best_score=score_exact(cur) # maybe fast for n large? okay
   # if cur_score >= best_score? 
   v=to_v(best); best_v=v
   beta_stages = [10,30,100,300,800]
   for beta in beta_stages:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       v_current=best_v.copy()
       # maybe if current exact score already good, still.
       prev_val = soft_obj(v_current,beta)
       while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
           res = minimize(...)
           v_new = res.x
           # center v_new? optional
           x_new=from_v(v_new)
           sc_fast=score_fast(x_new)
           if not finite: v_current=best_v; break
           if res.fun <= prev_val + 1e-8:
               v_current = v_new
               prev_val = res.fun
               if sc_fast < best_score - 1e-8:
                   sc_exact=score_exact(x_new)
                   if sc_exact < best_score:
                       best= x_new; best_score=sc_exact; best_v=v_new
           else:
               # revert?
               break
           if res.status not max_iters: break  # scipy constant? We'll define status acceptance.
       # after beta stage, update best_v? If no exact improvement but v_current may be better for high beta? set best_v? Maybe keep best_v if exact improved; else keep previous. But LSE at low beta may move in direction useful. We can set best_v = preserve? Hmm. If low beta moved away but exact not improve, high beta from best_v likely better. Keep best_v exact best. ```

Let's implement soft cache with a small class? No lambdas, but class okay? Simpler dict:
```
_SOFT_CACHE = {"v": None, "beta": None, "val": None, "grad": None}

def _soft_store(v,beta,val,grad):
   _SOFT_CACHE["v"]=v.copy()
...
def _soft_match(v,beta):
   sv=_SOFT_CACHE["v"]
   if sv is None: return False
   if len(sv)!=len(v): return False
   if abs(float(beta)-float(_SOFT_CACHE["beta"]))>1e-12: return False
   return bool(np.array_equal(sv, v))
```
If v contains nan, array_equal false; recompute. But if v is list of floats, store np array. When jac called, v np array; equal values. Good.

Now, performance: In `soft_fjac`, each call centers v: `v_centered = v - np.max(v)`? For objective, p = exp(v - v_max). But gradient formula uses p only, not v. However for line search, the JVP? The gradient wrt original v is same as wrt centered variables only if centered shift depends on max(v); adding constant to all v leaves objective unchanged, gradient sum zero. The stable softmax gradient formula is valid wrt original v. If we use v_shift = v - c where c=max(v) (non-smooth due max), the function composed with shift has same value but gradient? f(v - c(v)). If c chosen max, the operation is just subtracting a scalar; v_shift not a differentiable transform. But for computing softmax, using p from v is fine; formula for gradient wrt v uses p. We don't need v_shift in derivative. In soft_fjac, we can compute vmax = v.max(); p=exp(v-vmax)/sum; x=T p; grad formula. This is correct for original v. The stored cache compares original v. Good. No need to store v_shift.

If v has huge values, `v-vmax` okay; if vmax=-1e308? exp of small? If all very negative, vmax finite, differences <=0. If v includes -inf, exp=0; okay maybe. Could clip v to [-700,700] to avoid -inf? If v finite but very negative, difference may be <-745, exp underflow 0, p may become zero for many. Softmax still okay unless all underflow? vmax ensures at least one p positive. Large gaps cause some zeros; objective maybe okay. Gradient for zero p coordinates: grad=2*x*(h - p Wx)=0 because x=0; line search won't move them, potentially stuck if coordinate should become positive. To allow zeros to become positive, gradient should be? If x_i=0 exactly, softmax x_i>0 not zero unless underflow. p_i underflow to 0 if difference<-745; then grad 0, cannot increase. To avoid, clip differences to -50? Or use bounds? We can if vmax finite, subtract maybe v - (vmax - 50)? That changes relative? Need not. Use stable and ensure exponent arg > -50 for coordinates? If we clip lower to -50, p_i becomes e^-50 for very negative, so gradient nonzero. This biases but helps. In softmax, exact with huge differences, exact p ~0; no need to move from zero if truly zero. `anneal_polish` implementation:
```
def anneal_polish(x, deadline, rng):
   x=normalize_T(x); cur=x; cur_score=score_fast(cur); best=cur; best_score=cur_score
   sigma=0.15; Ttemp=0.01; no_improve=0; step=0
   while time < deadline-0.5:
      cand=cur.copy()
      nb=int(rng.integers(1,3))
      n=cand.size
      for _ in range(nb):
        width=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//8+1)))
        start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
        scale=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,width))
        cand[start:start+width]*=scale
      if rng.random()<0.2:
        cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
      cand=normalize_T(cand)
      sc=score_fast(cand)
      if finite:
         if sc <= cur_score:
            cur=cand; cur_score=sc; no_improve=0
         else:
            if rng.random()<math.exp(-max(0,sc-cur_score)/Ttemp): accept...
            else no_improve++
         if sc<best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc
      else no_improve++
      if no_improve>80: cur=best; cur_score=best_score; sigma*=0.7; Ttemp*=0.7; no_improve=0
      step%=100: sigma*=0.98; Ttemp*=0.98
   # exact best
   return best, score_exact(best)
```
Use fast for acceptance; final exact. May waste.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_length(n, parent, deadline, rng):
    best_x_local = None
    # create candidates
    cands = generate_candidates(n, parent, rng)
    scored=[]
    for cand in cands:
       x=normalize_T(cand); sc=score_fast(x); scored.append((sc,x))
    scored.sort
    # Main candidate = scored[0]
    budget_main = min(0.6*available, deadline-time-10)
    x=full_refine_scored(scored[0], deadline_main)
    local best
    # Secondary if available and time: maybe scored[1]
    for each remaining with decreasing budget:
       if time < deadline - 20:
          x=full_refine(candidate, time+min(budget_secondary, ...))
          update
    return local best
```

`central_seq(n)`:
```
n=int(n)
if n<=0: return np.array([])
a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1
for i in range(n-1): a[i+1]=a[i]*(i+0.5)/(i+1)
return a
```

`clean_array(x)`:
```
x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
if x.size==0: return x
x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=UB_FINAL, neginf=0.0)
return np.clip(x,0.0,UB_FINAL)
```

`normalize_T(x, n=None)`:
```
x=clean_array(x)
if x.size==0: return x
n=x.size
s=float(np.sum(x))
if s <= 1e-300:
    x=np.ones(n)
    s=float(n)
T=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
return x*(T/s)
```

`eval_score_exact(x)`:
```
x=clean_array(x); n=x.size
if n==0: inf
s=float(np.sum(x))
if s <0.01: inf
c=np.convolve(x,x)
m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else inf
val=2.0*n*m/(s*s)
return val if finite else inf
```

`conv_fast_full(x)`:
```
x=clean_array(x); n=x.size
if n<=0: return empty
L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
fx=np.fft.rfft(x,n=L)
c=np.fft.irfft(fx*fx,n=L)[:2*n-1]
c=c.real
c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
return c
```

`score_fast(x)`:

`soft_obj(v,beta)` with cache:
```
def soft_obj(v,beta):
    vraw=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
    val,grad=soft_fjac(vraw,float(beta))
    _SOFT_CACHE['v']=vraw.copy()
    _SOFT_CACHE['beta']=float(beta)
    _SOFT_CACHE['val']=val
    _SOFT_CACHE['grad']=grad
    return val
```
`soft_grad` similar. Need if vraw contains large; okay.

`lp_refine_loop`:
```
def lp_refine_loop(x, deadline, max_iters=50, rng=None):
   x=normalize_T(x); best_x=x; best_score=eval_score_exact(x); best_fast=score_fast(x)
   last_y=None
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 25 (if n>1024? maybe 15): break
       x_clean = sanitize_for_lp(x)
       M = max_conv_exact(x_clean)
       if not finite: break
       y = lp_direction(x_clean, M, 0.0, deadline)
       if y is None:
          if it<2: y=lp_direction(x_clean,M,0.01,deadline)
          else break
       if y is None: break
       last_y=y
       z, z_fast = lp_line_search(x_clean, y)
       if z is not None and z_fast < best_fast - 1e-9:
          z_exact=eval_score_exact(z)
          if z_exact < best_score - 1e-10:
              best_x=z; best_score=z_exact; best_fast=z_fast; no_improve=0
          else: no_improve +=1
       else: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve >= 6: break
   return best_x,best_score,last_y
```
Use `x` for line search rather than x_clean? `x` may have tiny zeros, x_clean positive. If line search starts from x_clean, best_x sum T and positive; okay. It may alter x slightly by floor; best_score with x_clean maybe slightly not exact from original. Use x_clean as current to maintain feasibility. If it improves, best_x=x_clean. If no improvement, but x_clean might have different score? We should update best if x_clean score lower? Probably not. Use `x_work = sanitize_for_lp(x)` and if its score更好的 update. Good.

But adding floor to every LP iteration may gradually remove zeros and change shape; okay.

Now, `full_refine_sequence`:
```
def full_refine_sequence(x, deadline, rng, tag=''):
    x=normalize_T(x)
    best_x=x; best_score=eval_score_exact(x)
    total=max(0,deadline-time)
    if total<10: return best_x,best_score
    t0=time
    # smooth1
    d1=t0+0.35*total
    if d1 > start+8:
       x1,sc1=smooth_refine(x,d1,rng)
       if sc1<best_score: best...
    # lp1
    d2=d1+0.30*total
    if linprog and time < d2-20:
       x2,sc2,_=lp_refine_loop(x,d2, max_iters=30, rng)
       if sc2<best_score: best...
    # smooth2
    d3=d2+0.25*total
    if time < d3-8:
       x3,sc3=smooth_refine(x,d3,rng)
       update
    # final anneal or lp if time
    if time < deadline-5:
       x4,sc4=anneal_polish(x,deadline,rng)

def generate_candidates(n,parent,best_x,rng):
    cands=[]
    c=central_seq(n)
    cands.append(c)
    if parent is not None and size>0: cands.append(interpolate_sequence(parent,n))
    if best_x is not None and size>0 and (parent is None or size best != parent size): cands.append(interpolate_sequence(best_x,n))
    # add transforms of central
    c_norm=clean(c)/sum
    cands.append(c_norm[::-1])
    cands.append(0.5*(c_norm+c_norm[::-1]))
    # power alpha
    for alpha in (0.35,0.45,0.75): # maybe 0.4,0.5
       i=np.arange(1,n+1); cands.append(np.power(i,-alpha))
    # central blend with rev/uniform/slope
    c_norm = c/sum(c)
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for lam in (0.25,0.5,0.75): cands.append((1-lam)*c_norm + lam*c_norm[::-1])
    for slope in (-0.4,0.4):
       a=np.maximum(c_norm*(1+slope*(t-0.5)),1e-10); cands.append(a)
    # noise
    for sig in (0.1,0.25): cands.append(c_norm*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,n)))
    # if parent length n, reverse parent
    if parent is not None and size==n: cands.append(clean(parent)[::-1])
    # unique by first few? not needed.
    return [normalize_T(a) for a in cands if finite]

`_soft_match`:
```
def _soft_match(v, beta):
    sv = _SOFT_CACHE.get("v")
    if sv is None: return False
    bv = _SOFT_CACHE.get("beta")
    if bv is None: return False
    if len(sv) != len(v): return False
    if abs(bv - float(beta)) > 1e-12: return False
    return bool(np.array_equal(sv, v))
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for nonnegative convolution peak-factor sequences.

The search combines:
- FFT-based softmax optimization of a log-sum-exp approximation,
- state-of-the-art Frank-Wolfe style LP moves (maximize sum g subject to
  cross-convolution bound),
- candidate generation, line searches, and light simulated annealing.

The objective is 2 * n * max(a * a) / sum(a)^2.  Internally we usually
normalize the vector to sum sqrt(2n), where the objective equals the
maximum convolution value.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix
except Exception:
    coo_matrix = None

UB_FINAL = 1000.0
_LP_MAX_N = 1536
_SOFT_CACHE = {"v": None, "beta": None, "val": None, "grad": None}


def central_seq(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (i + 0.5) / (i + 1.0)
    return a


def clean_array(x):
    """Convert to 1-D float64 and sanitize like the evaluator."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=UB_FINAL, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, UB_FINAL)


def normalize_T(x):
    """Normalize a sequence to sum sqrt(2n), the LP/softmax working scale."""
    x = clean_array(x)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.size)
    T = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(x.size))
    return x * (T / s)


def eval_score_exact(x):
    """Exact score, compatible with the provided evaluator for valid ranges."""
    x = clean_array(x)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(x, x)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    val = float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def conv_fast_full(x):
    """Fast full linear convolution via FFT."""
    x = clean_array(x)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fx * fx, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c.real, 0.0)
    return c


def score_fast(x):
    """Fast score evaluation, exact enough for search."""
    x = clean_array(x)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if n <= 256:
        c = np.convolve(x, x)
    else:
        c = conv_fast_full(x)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    val = float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def max_conv_exact(x):
    """Maximum coefficient of x*x using direct convolution."""
    x = clean_array(x)
    if x.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(x, x)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    return m if math.isfinite(m) else float("inf")


def sort_by_score(item):
    """Sort helper for (score, sequence) pairs."""
    return item[0]


def to_v(x):
    """Convert a positive sequence to softmax log-parameters."""
    x = clean_array(x)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-30)
    v = np.log(x)
    if v.size:
        v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return v


def from_v(v):
    """Convert softmax parameters back to a sum-sqrt(2n) sequence."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.full(n, math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    T = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    arg = np.clip(v - vmax, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    es = float(np.sum(e))
    if not math.isfinite(es) or es <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        es = float(n)
    p = e / es
    p = p / float(np.sum(p))
    return T * p


def soft_fjac(v, beta):
    """
    Smooth softmax objective and gradient.

    For x = T * softmax(v), T^2 = 2n, the exact score is max(x*x).
    This returns a log-sum-exp approximation and its gradient wrt v.
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    T = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))

    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    arg = np.clip(v - vmax, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    es = float(np.sum(e))
    if not math.isfinite(es) or es <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = e / es
    p = p / float(np.sum(p))
    x = T * p

    L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()

    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, n=L)
    c_full = np.fft.irfft(fx * fx, n=L)
    c = c_full[:2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not math.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    z = beta * (c - cm)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    ez_sum = float(np.sum(ez))
    if not math.isfinite(ez_sum) or ez_sum <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    val = cm + math.log(ez_sum) / beta
    w = ez / ez_sum

    # h_i = sum_j x_j w_{i+j}, computed by circular convolution with reversed x.
    ar = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    ar[:n] = x[::-1]
    far = np.fft.rfft(ar, n=L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(far * fw, n=L)
    h = h_full[n - 1:2 * n - 1].real
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    hx = float(np.dot(h, x))
    grad = 2.0 * x * (h - p * hx)
    # The softmax gradient should be orthogonal to the all-ones direction.
    if grad.size:
        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return float(val), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def _soft_store(v, beta, val, grad):
    _SOFT_CACHE["v"] = np.array(v, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    _SOFT_CACHE["beta"] = float(beta)
    _SOFT_CACHE["val"] = float(val)
    _SOFT_CACHE["grad"] = np.array(grad, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)


def _soft_match(v, beta):
    sv = _SOFT_CACHE.get("v")
    if sv is None:
        return False
    sb = _SOFT_CACHE.get("beta")
    if sb is None:
        return False
    if len(sv) != len(v):
        return False
    if abs(float(sb) - float(beta)) > 1e-12:
        return False
    return bool(np.array_equal(sv, v))


def soft_obj(v, beta):
    vraw = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    val, grad = soft_fjac(vraw, float(beta))
    _soft_store(vraw, beta, val, grad)
    return val


def soft_grad(v, beta):
    vraw = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if _soft_match(vraw, beta):
        return _SOFT_CACHE["grad"]
    val, grad = soft_fjac(vraw, float(beta))
    _soft_store(vraw, beta, val, grad)
    return grad


def run_lbfgs_chunk(v, beta, maxiter, deadline):
    """Run a bounded-time chunk of L-BFGS-B on the smooth softmax objective."""
    if minimize is None:
        return v, None, True, True
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if v.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return v, None, True, True
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    maxiter = int(max(1, maxiter))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun=soft_obj,
            x0=v,
            args=(float(beta),),
            jac=soft_grad,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxls": 40,
                "ftol": 1e-11,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        vx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return v, None, True, True

    if vx.size != v.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
        return v, None, True, False

    vx = vx - float(np.mean(vx))
    val = float(res.fun) if getattr(res, "fun", None) is not None else None
    niter = int(getattr(res, "niter", 0))
    converged = bool(niter < maxiter)
    return vx, val, converged, False


def smooth_refine(x, deadline, rng):
    """Multi-stage L-BFGS-B refinement on the smooth softmax model."""
    rng = rng if rng is not None else np.random.default_rng(0)
    v0 = to_v(x)
    x0 = normalize_T(from_v(v0))
    best_x = x0.copy()
    best_score = eval_score_exact(best_x)
    best_v = v0.copy()
    best_fast = score_fast(best_x)

    total = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if total < 6.0:
        return best_x, best_score

    n = x0.size
    if n <= 512:
        betas = (20.0, 60.0, 150.0, 400.0, 1000.0)
        chunk = 100
    elif n <= 1024:
        betas = (10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 800.0)
        chunk = 70
    elif n <= 2048:
        betas = (8.0, 25.0, 80.0, 250.0, 600.0)
        chunk = 45
    else:
        betas = (6.0, 20.0, 60.0, 180.0, 450.0)
        chunk = 25

    path_v = best_v.copy()

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 3.0:
            break

        # Continue from a path that looks competitive, otherwise from best exact.
        if path_v.size == best_v.size:
            pv_x = from_v(path_v)
            pv_score = score_fast(pv_x)
            if math.isfinite(pv_score) and pv_score <= best_fast + 0.15:
                vwork = path_v.copy()
            else:
                vwork = best_v.copy()
        else:
            vwork = best_v.copy()

        vwork = np.nan_to_num(vwork, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        vwork = vwork - float(np.mean(vwork))
        prev_val = soft_obj(vwork, beta)
        if not math.isfinite(prev_val):
            prev_val = 1e100

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
            vnew, val, converged, failed = run_lbfgs_chunk(vwork, beta, chunk, deadline)
            if failed:
                break
            if val is None or val > prev_val + 1e-7:
                break

            vnew = np.asarray(vnew, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            vnew = np.nan_to_num(vnew, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            vnew = vnew - float(np.mean(vnew))
            xnew = normalize_T(from_v(vnew))
            sfast = score_fast(xnew)

            if not math.isfinite(sfast):
                break

            if sfast < best_fast:
                sexact = eval_score_exact(xnew)
                if math.isfinite(sexact) and sexact < best_score:
                    best_x = xnew.copy()
                    best_score = sexact
                    best_v = vnew.copy()
                    best_fast = sfast
                    path_v = vnew.copy()

            if sfast <= best_fast + 0.10:
                path_v = vnew.copy()

            vwork = vnew
            prev_val = float(val)

            if converged:
                break

    if path_v.size == best_x.size:
        xpath = normalize_T(from_v(path_v))
        sfast = score_fast(xpath)
        if math.isfinite(sfast) and sfast <= best_fast + 0.05:
            sexact = eval_score_exact(xpath)
            if math.isfinite(sexact) and sexact < best_score:
                best_x = xpath.copy()
                best_score = sexact

    return best_x, best_score


def interpolate_sequence(a, n):
    """Resample a sequence to length n, normalized to sum sqrt(2n)."""
    a = clean_array(a)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return normalize_T(central_seq(n))

    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return normalize_T(central_seq(n))

    if a.size == n:
        return a * (math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)) / s)

    old = a / s
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.interp(new_x, old_x, old)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 1e-30)
    tsum = float(np.sum(vals))
    if tsum <= 0:
        return normalize_T(central_seq(n))
    return vals * (math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)) / tsum)


def make_candidates(n, parent, best_x, rng):
    """Generate diverse starting vectors for a fixed length."""
    rng = rng if rng is not None else np.random.default_rng(0)
    c = central_seq(n)
    if c.size == 0:
        c = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cs = float(np.sum(c))
    if cs <= 0:
        c = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        cs = float(n)
    cn = c / cs

    cands = [cn]
    if parent is not None:
        cands.append(interpolate_sequence(parent, n))
    if best_x is not None:
        cands.append(interpolate_sequence(best_x, n))

    cands.append(cn[::-1].copy())
    cands.append(0.5 * (cn + cn[::-1]))

    if n <= 2048:
        for alpha in (0.35, 0.45, 0.60):
            i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            cands.append(np.power(i, -alpha))
        for lam in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
            cands.append((1.0 - lam) * cn + lam * cn[::-1])
        tvec = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        for slope in (-0.6, 0.6):
            a = cn * (1.0 + slope * (tvec - 0.5))
            cands.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-10))
        for sig in (0.08, 0.20):
            a = cn * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n).astype(np.float64))
            cands.append(a)
    else:
        cands.append(0.6 * cn + 0.4 * cn[::-1])
        a = cn * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n).astype(np.float64))
        cands.append(a)

    out = []
    for cand in cands:
        x = normalize_T(cand)
        if x.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            out.append(x)

    if not out:
        out.append(normalize_T(c))
    return out


def sanitize_for_lp(x):
    """Mildly densify a vector for the LP to avoid unbounded/degenerate cases."""
    x = normalize_T(x)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    floor = 1e-8
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    T = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    return x * (T / float(np.sum(x)))


def lp_direction(x, M, deadline, relax=0.0):
    """
    State-of-the-art LP move.

    Solve max sum y, y >= 0, x*y <= M*(1+relax).  Return y normalized to sum T.
    """
    if linprog is None or coo_matrix is None:
        return None
    n = int(x.size)
    if n < 2 or n > _LP_MAX_N:
        return None
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
        return None

    x = sanitize_for_lp(x)
    n = x.size
    T = float(np.sum(x))
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    max_x = float(np.max(x)) if x.size else 1.0
    U = min(1000.0, max(100.0, 10.0 * max_x))

    b = float(M) * (1.0 + float(relax) + 1e-9) + 1e-9

    rows = []
    cols = []
    data = []

    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        if k < n:
            m = k + 1
            cols.append(np.arange(m, dtype=np.int32))
            data.append(x[k::-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False))
        else:
            m = 2 * n - 1 - k
            cols.append(np.arange(n - m, n, dtype=np.int32))
            data.append(x[n - 1::-1][:m].astype(np.float64, copy=False))
        rows.append(np.full(m, k, dtype=np.int32))

    if not rows:
        return None

    row_arr = np.concatenate(rows)
    col_arr = np.concatenate(cols)
    data_arr = np.concatenate(data)

    try:
        A = coo_matrix((data_arr, (row_arr, col_arr)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n)).tocsr()
        time_left = max(1.0, min(45.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)))
        res = linprog(
            -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, b, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=(0.0, float(U)),
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": time_left, "presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None

    y = clean_array(res.x)
    if y.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return None

    sy = float(np.sum(y))
    if not math.isfinite(sy) or sy <= T * (1.0 - 1e-6):
        return None

    y = y * (T / sy)

    # Ignore directions indistinguishable from the current vector.
    if np.linalg.norm(y - x) <= 1e-5 * T:
        return None

    return y


def lp_line_search(x, y):
    """Line search from x toward an LP direction y; uses fast score selection."""
    x = normalize_T(x)
    y = normalize_T(y)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0 or y.size != n:
        return x, score_fast(x)

    base_fast = score_fast(x)
    best_z = x.copy()
    best_fast = base_fast

    alphas = (1.0, 0.75, 0.55, 0.4, 0.3, 0.22, 0.16, 0.11, 0.08, 0.05, 0.03, 0.015, 0.0075, 0.003)
    for a in alphas:
        z = (1.0 - a) * x + a * y
        z = normalize_T(z)
        sc = score_fast(z)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_fast:
            best_fast = sc
            best_z = z.copy()

    # If the pure endpoint was promising but fast evaluation is noisy, explicitly test it.
    sc_pure = score_fast(y)
    if math.isfinite(sc_pure) and sc_pure < best_fast:
        best_fast = sc_pure
        best_z = y.copy()

    return best_z, best_fast


def lp_refine_loop(x, deadline, max_iters, rng):
    """Iterative LP + line search.  Returns (best_x, exact_score, last_y)."""
    x = normalize_T(x)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = eval_score_exact(best_x)
    best_fast = score_fast(best_x)
    last_y = None
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max(1, max_iters))):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 25.0:
            break

        xwork = sanitize_for_lp(x)
        sw = score_fast(xwork)
        if math.isfinite(sw):
            sw_exact = eval_score_exact(xwork)
            if math.isfinite(sw_exact) and sw_exact < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_x = xwork.copy()
                best_score = sw_exact
                best_fast = sw

        M = max_conv_exact(xwork)
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        y = lp_direction(xwork, M, deadline, 0.0)
        if y is None:
            # A small relaxation can sometimes escape a very tight local point.
            if no_improve < 4:
                y = lp_direction(xwork, M, deadline, 0.01)
            else:
                break

        if y is None:
            break

        last_y = y
        z, zfast = lp_line_search(xwork, y)
        if math.isfinite(zfast) and zfast < best_fast - 1e-9:
            zexact = eval_score_exact(z)
            if math.isfinite(zexact) and zexact < best_score - 1e-10:
                best_x = z.copy()
                best_score = zexact
                best_fast = zfast
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 6:
            break

    return best_x, best_score, last_y


def anneal_polish(x, deadline, rng):
    """Light stochastic block-multiplicative polishing."""
    rng = rng if rng is not None else np.random.default_rng(0)
    x = normalize_T(x)
    cur = x.copy()
    cur_score = score_fast(cur)
    best = x.copy()
    best_score = eval_score_exact(best)

    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        fbb = normalize_T(central_seq(x.size if x.size > 0 else 1024))
        cur = fbb.copy()
        best = fbb.copy()
        cur_score = score_fast(cur)
        best_score = eval_score_exact(best)

    n = cur.size
    sigma = 0.14
    Ttemp = 0.008
    no_improve = 0
    step = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
        step += 1
        cand = cur.copy()

        nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 3))
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            max_w = max(1, n // 10)
            width = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, max_w + 1)))
            width = min(n, width)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            scale = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width).astype(np.float64))
            cand[start:start + width] *= scale

        if rng.random() < 0.17:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = normalize_T(cand)
        sc = score_fast(cand)

        if math.isfinite(sc):
            if sc <= cur_score:
                cur = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                diff = sc - cur_score
                if diff < 700.0 * Ttemp:
                    pacc = math.exp(-diff / max(Ttemp, 1e-9))
                    if rng.random() < pacc:
                        cur = cand.copy()
                        cur_score = sc
                        no_improve = 0
                    else:
                        no_improve += 1
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

            if sc < best_score:
                sexact = eval_score_exact(cand)
                if math.isfinite(sexact) and sexact < best_score:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sexact
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 100:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_score = score_fast(cur)
            no_improve = 0
            sigma = max(0.02, 0.7 * sigma)
            Ttemp = max(0.0005, 0.7 * Ttemp)

        if step % 60 == 0:
            sigma = max(0.02, 0.97 * sigma)
            Ttemp = max(0.0003, 0.97 * Ttemp)

    return best, best_score


def full_refine_sequence(x, deadline, rng):
    """Complete polishing pipeline for one candidate sequence."""
    x = normalize_T(x)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = eval_score_exact(best_x)
    cur = x.copy()

    total = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if total < 8.0:
        return best_x, best_score

    now = time.monotonic()
    d1 = now + 0.35 * total
    if d1 > now + 6.0:
        x1, sc1 = smooth_refine(cur, d1, rng)
        if math.isfinite(sc1):
            if sc1 < best_score:
                best_x, best_score = x1, sc1
            if sc1 <= best_score + 1e-7:
                cur = x1

    d2 = d1 + 0.30 * total
    if linprog is not None and time.monotonic() < d2 - 18.0 and cur.size <= _LP_MAX_N:
        x2, sc2, _ = lp_refine_loop(cur, min(d2, deadline - 2.0), 45, rng)
        if math.isfinite(sc2):
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best_x, best_score = x2, sc2
            if sc2 <= best_score + 1e-7:
                cur = x2

    d3 = d2 + 0.25 * total
    if time.monotonic() < d3 - 6.0:
        x3, sc3 = smooth_refine(cur, min(d3, deadline - 2.0), rng)
        if math.isfinite(sc3):
            if sc3 < best_score:
                best_x, best_score = x3, sc3
            if sc3 <= best_score + 1e-7:
                cur = x3

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
        x4, sc4 = anneal_polish(cur, deadline - 1.0, rng)
        if math.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_score:
            best_x, best_score = x4, sc4

    return best_x, best_score


def optimize_length(n, parent, best_x, budget_deadline, rng):
    """Optimize several candidates at a fixed length within a deadline."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_x, float("inf")

    local_best = best_x if best_x is not None else normalize_T(central_seq(n))
    local_score = eval_score_exact(local_best) if local_best is not None else float("inf")

    remaining_total = max(0.0, budget_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining_total < 20.0:
        return local_best, local_score

    cand_list = make_candidates(n, parent, best_x, rng)
    scored = []
    for cand in cand_list:
        sc = score_fast(cand)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, cand))
    if not scored:
        return local_best, local_score

    scored.sort(key=sort_by_score)

    if len(scored) >= 3:
        fracs = (0.52, 0.28, 0.20)
        candidates = scored[:3]
    elif len(scored) == 2:
        fracs = (0.65, 0.35)
        candidates = scored[:2]
    else:
        fracs = (1.0,)
        candidates = scored[:1]

    for idx, item in enumerate(candidates):
        if time.monotonic() >= budget_deadline - 12.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[idx]
        phase_budget = frac * remaining_total
        phase_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, phase_budget)
        if phase_deadline < time.monotonic() + 12.0:
            break

        x, sc = full_refine_sequence(item[1], min(phase_deadline, budget_deadline - 5.0), rng)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
            local_best = x.copy()
            local_score = sc

    return local_best, local_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400):
    """
    Search for a low-score nonnegative sequence within the supplied time budget.
    Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    try:
        start = time.monotonic()
    except Exception:
        start = 0.0

    try:
        total = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        total = 400.0

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    hard_deadline = start + total - 4.0
    soft_deadline = hard_deadline - 18.0
    soft_budget = max(30.0, soft_deadline - start)

    # Safe baseline.
    fallback = normalize_T(central_seq(1024))
    best_x = fallback.copy()
    best_score = eval_score_exact(best_x)

    # Quick central baselines at several lengths.
    for n in (64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        try:
            x = normalize_T(central_seq(n))
            scf = score_fast(x)
            if math.isfinite(scf) and scf < best_score:
                scx = eval_score_exact(x)
                if math.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_score:
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    best_score = scx
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Use the supplied previous construction if present and valid.
    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if hs.size > 0:
            x = normalize_T(hs)
            scf = score_fast(x)
            if math.isfinite(scf) and scf < best_score:
                scx = eval_score_exact(x)
                if math.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_score:
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    best_score = scx
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"initial best_score={best_score:.10f} n={best_x.size}", flush=True)

    # Primary search lengths.  1024 is emphasized because the prior construction
    # and target are in that regime, but larger length is also searched.
    base_specs = [(1024, 0.46), (2048, 0.28), (512, 0.16), (4096, 0.10)]

    # If the current best already has a different useful length, search it first.
    order = []
    current_len = int(best_x.size)
    for n, frac in base_specs:
        order.append((n, frac))
    if current_len not in [n for n, _ in order] and 128 <= current_len <= 8192:
        order.insert(0, (current_len, 0.20))

    for n, frac in order:
        if time.monotonic() >= soft_deadline - 18.0:
            break
        budget = min(frac * soft_budget, soft_deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0)
        if budget < 30.0:
            continue
        n = int(n)
        if n < 16 or n > 8192:
            continue
        try:
            x, sc = optimize_length(n, best_x, best_x, time.monotonic() + budget, rng)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_x = x.copy()
                best_score = sc
            print(f"length n={n:5d} best_score={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"length n={n} failed: {exc}", flush=True)

    # Use remaining time to polish the current best length.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 12.0:
        try:
            x, sc = full_refine_sequence(
                best_x,
                hard_deadline - 3.0,
                rng,
            )
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_x = x.copy()
                best_score = sc
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"final polish failed: {exc}", flush=True)

    # Final sanitization.
    best_x = clean_array(best_x).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    if best_x.size == 0:
        best_x = normalize_T(central_seq(1024))

    final_score = eval_score_exact(best_x)
    if not math.isfinite(final_score) or final_score >= best_score - 1e-9:
        # If comparison was affected by fast caches, still keep a valid sequence.
        if not math.isfinite(final_score):
            best_x = normalize_T(central_seq(1024))
            final_score = eval_score_exact(best_x)

    # Optional consistency check with the authoritative evaluator if present.
    try:
        official = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in best_x]))
        if math.isfinite(official):
            print(f"official evaluator score={official:.10f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL best_score={final_score:.10f} n={best_x.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(v) for v in best_x]
```
